- **knowledge-extractor**: Read-only map-phase extractor for bulk knowledge-base ingest. Reads ONE source, emits a compact structured JSON extraction — findings, statistics, citations, confidence, and proposed target library files — and never writes any library file. Lightweight and Haiku-capable: mechanical extraction, not synthesis. Dispatched many-wide by kb-ingest-bulk; the agent-knowledge-updater does the synthesis in the reduce phase. - **knowledge-extractor**: EXTRACT stable knowledge from completed code changes into structured WAL fragments. Writes ONLY the dimensions (Domain, API, Rules, Data, Architecture) the user elected via `h-archive` Step 3b — never writes a fragment for an unselected dimension. TRIGGER during Phase 6 Archive of STANDARD tasks after the user multi-select, OR on user request for milestone WAL flush. NOT for: wiki index merging (use `librarian`), index splitting (use `knowledge-architect`), free-form doc authoring (use `documentation-curator`). Returns written WAL fragment file paths + per-dimension PASS/SKIP status. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/knowledge-extractor.md) · ★18)

Builds exactly ONE design direction from a research brief as a live route of the project (a scratch/playground route), checks that it renders, and reports the route. Several run in parallel, one per direction. Use from the koala-poc-canvas skill. ([jespinosa19/koala-poc-canvas](https://github.com/jespinosa19/koala-poc-canvas/blob/HEAD/skill/agents/koala-poc-canvas-builder.md) · ★4)
- **koala-poc-canvas-researcher**: Koala POC Canvas research agent. Before a round of design variants is built, reads the screen's code, the design system, the canvas history (verdicts, decisions, comments, drawings, requests) and the round's question, and writes a brief with 3 or 4 genuinely different directions to a file. Read-only apart from that one file. Use from the koala-poc-canvas skill, once per round. ([jespinosa19/koala-poc-canvas](https://github.com/jespinosa19/koala-poc-canvas/blob/HEAD/skill/agents/koala-poc-canvas-researcher.md) · ★4)
- **koala-poc-canvas-reviewer**: Koala POC Canvas review agent. Supervises a round before it reaches the owner: renders every built variant, checks each against the research brief (renders cleanly, faithful to its direction, honours the owner's comments and drawings, uses the design system) and that the set is genuinely different. Returns pass or fix per variant. Read-only. Use from the koala-poc-canvas skill after the builders finish. ([jespinosa19/koala-poc-canvas](https://github.com/jespinosa19/koala-poc-canvas/blob/HEAD/skill/agents/koala-poc-canvas-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **kobold-kernel-ggml**: Implements KoboldAI cognitive primitives (Story Management, Generation Pipeline, World Building, Agent Orchestration) as pure C/C++ tensor-based kernel libraries using GGML and llama.cpp backends, optimized for real-time collaborative storytelling and AI-assisted writing. ([OzCog/opencog-unified](https://github.com/OzCog/opencog-unified/blob/HEAD/hypermind/.github/agents/kobold-kernel-ggml.md) · ★3 · 10 copies)
- **kobold-reviewer-newhire**: Adversarial code review persona. Invoked by codedungeon code-review as one of four parallel critic subagents. Persona: 'First day, zero context, I am reading this.' Catches architectural/readability/contract violations that detail-focused critics miss. Output is JSON only. ([Overstrider/codedungeon](https://github.com/Overstrider/codedungeon/blob/HEAD/src/codedungeon/internal/prompts/files/agents/kobold-reviewer-newhire.md) · ★2)

- **kockgranskare**: Granskar ETT recepts tidsordning som en restaurangkock: kritisk väg, dödtid, sena starter, sådant som kallnar, hygienbyten och kontextbyten. - **kod-gozden-gecirici**: Adım sonu diff incelemesi — tenant izolasyonu, katman ihlali, hata yönetimi, sır sızıntısı. Yazma yetkisi yoktur; bulguları rapor eder. Büyük diff'lerin ana context'i şişirmesini engeller. ([paradoksix/ozelmh](https://github.com/paradoksix/ozelmh/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kod-gozden-gecirici.md))
- **kod-gozden-gecirici**: Arka uç (.NET) değişikliğini CLAUDE.md ve .claude/rules mühendislik kurallarına göre gözden geçirir ve bulguları önem derecesi ve dosya:satır ile raporlar; dosya değiştirmez. Bir özellik veya hata düzeltmesi bittikten sonra, commit önermeden önce kullan. Çağırırken değişen dosya listesini (git status/diff çıktısı) ve işin kısa amacını ver. - **kod-inceleyici**: Sustable Kod İnceleyici — bir işin tüm depolardaki diff'ini (iş dalı vs taban dalı) bağımsız gözle inceler: doğruluk ve KK karşılığı, sözleşmeye uyum, yetki/IDOR, veri bütünlüğü ve migration, performans, depo kuralları (soft-delete, veri kilidi, barrel, i18n), test yeterliliği. Backend değişikliğinde yetki-denetcisi kontrol listesini uygular. Dosyalara yazmaz (yalnız `07-inceleme.md`); bulgu raporu döner. Use after QA passes on an office job, before asking the user to approve commit/merge. Les diff og segir RÉTT AÐFERÐ eða RÖNG AÐFERÐ. Kallaðu á hana áður en nýr patch er skrifaður; deploy bíða ekki eftir henni — hún fer yfir þau eftir á. - **konte-staging-critic**: Critique pending konte panel compositions against their action, plates and neighbouring shots before generation. Given a target and engagement type, it resolves the PiRanha-style deployment plan (tools/agent_router.py) and fans out the specialist subagents in strix style — parallel within a phase group, sequential across groups — then validates, chains, and hands off to /autoreport. Use to run a full multi-agent assessment of a target's attack surface. ([weixuan0110/kraken](https://github.com/weixuan0110/kraken/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/kraken-orchestrator.md))
- **kraken-page-analyst**: Analyses saved Kraken page exemplars (raw/ HTML + JSON) for a given bucket and documents exactly what data each page type holds — key fields, labels, ID shapes, table columns, JSON schema, and how to reach the page. Writes a self-contained findings section. Read-only; does not probe the live site. ([R4chC0dE/StonksInHand](https://github.com/R4chC0dE/StonksInHand/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kraken-page-analyst.md))
- **kraken-prober**: Read-only GET prober for the Kraken support portal. Use to traverse a specific account, bucket, or candidate endpoint and report which URL templates are reachable, their status/content-type, and the links/XHR hooks they expose. NEVER POSTs, NEVER mutates. Always routes requests through probe/safety.py. ([R4chC0dE/StonksInHand](https://github.com/R4chC0dE/StonksInHand/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kraken-prober.md))
- **kramme:a11y-auditor**: Use this agent to audit UI code for WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility issues, including semantics, ARIA, keyboard navigation, focus handling, screen reader support, and contrast. Use it when accessibility is an explicit requirement or when reviewing forms, dialogs, and other interactive flows; not for general UX or visual consistency review. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Aa11y-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:architecture-strategist**: Use this agent to review code changes from an architectural perspective, focusing on system boundaries, dependency direction, layering, abstractions, service interfaces, and alignment with existing patterns. Best for structural refactors, new services, or cross-cutting features; not for style-only or narrowly local implementation feedback. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Aarchitecture-strategist.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:cleanup-reviewer**: Use this agent to review recent or PR-scoped changes for code the change does not need to own or could express more simply, across four dimensions - lean (delete, replace with the standard library, native platform features, installed dependencies, or existing helpers, and drop YAGNI abstractions), removal (verify that deleted, deprecated, or consolidated code is safe to remove by tracing every reference), refactor (reuse, composition, and codebase fit), and simplify (clarity and maintainability while preserving exact behavior). Review-only inside a multi-reviewer run; may apply behavior-prese… ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Acleanup-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:code-reviewer**: Use this agent to review recent code against project guidelines, CLAUDE.md conventions, and established patterns. It is best used after writing or modifying code, especially before commits or PRs, and should be pointed at the relevant files or diff scope; not for deep product, accessibility, or performance-specific review. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Acode-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:codebase-pattern-reviewer**: Use this agent during spec or design review to detect whether a proposed implementation introduces new codebase patterns, conventions, dependencies, file structures, or abstractions without rationale. Best for pre-implementation SIW spec audits; not for line-level code review or implementation conformance checks. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Acodebase-pattern-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:comment-analyzer**: Use this agent to review code comments for accuracy, necessity, completeness, and long-term maintainability. Use it after adding or editing docstrings, inline comments, or PR documentation changes; not for reviewing code behavior unrelated to comments. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Acomment-analyzer.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:convention-drift-reviewer**: Use this agent to review implemented branch changes for convention drift and overcaution relative to the codebase's own established practice. It mines a peer-file baseline with quorum evidence before judging, flags new patterns, conventions, dependencies, or abstractions introduced without rationale, and flags code that is more defensive or complicated than comparable existing sites, citing exemplar file:line evidence or documented rules for every finding. Also supports a refute mode that adversarially re-checks findings against wider sampling. Not for spec-level pattern review (use kramme:co… ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Aconvention-drift-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:copy-reviewer**: Use this agent to review UI text for redundancy and remove labels, helper copy, tooltips, and instructions that merely restate what the interface already communicates. Use it for PRs or audits where copy minimalism matters; not for grammar, tone, brand voice, or broader UX review. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Acopy-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:design-iterator**: Use this agent when a design still feels off after an initial pass or two and needs iterative refinement. It reviews screenshots, applies improvements, and repeats for multiple rounds to improve hierarchy, spacing, color balance, and overall polish; not for one-off code changes that are already visually solid. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Adesign-iterator.md) · ★2)
- **kramme:deslop-reviewer**: Use this agent to detect AI-generated code slop in code changes or in review findings. In code-review mode it flags unnecessary comments, defensive noise, weak typing, and style inconsistencies in the diff; in meta-review mode it flags review suggestions that would introduce the same patterns. ([Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow](https://github.com/Abildtoft/kramme-cc-workflow/blob/HEAD/kramme-cc-workflow/agents/kramme%3Adeslop-reviewer.md) · ★2)
